| Notes | This is the 'descriptor' half of [WP-03.03](../../work-packages/03-contract-foundation-and-licence-split.md#rule-wp-03.03). Registry 04 explicitly allocates the missing initial wire fields; ContractVersion identity is separate from ContractCompatibility support minima. Their version/minimum strings use registry 04 section 3 explicit SemVer profile: 128 ASCII maximum, build metadata retained in exact identity but ignored by precedence; other Key fields remain unchanged. ContextProvider/ContextDescriptor belong to PublicApi; no Foundation-to-PublicApi import cycle. ApplicationPresence and DeviceCapabilityView remain CON.11, with no InstancePresence duplicate. EncodedBodyReader validates expected type/descriptor/snapshot, canonical expiry, <=64MiB and exact byteLength plus ResourceVersionRef contentHash before invoking a caller-supplied statically generated parser; it neither fetches nor authorizes content. No new dependencies or projects. Small and foundational: many later domain tasks (CON.06 in-process ports, CON.10 task/chat tool binding) reference CapabilityDescriptor for tool metadata, so this should land early even though nothing strictly blocks it from running in parallel with CON.03-CON.05. Security support is limited to actually observed generic-api-key false positives for CON.02 public SHA256 inventory entries; bind each exact containing path, JSON key and recomputed value to its observed commit, including necessary immutable historical snapshots. Rebase or normalization may update an exception only after an actual scan finding and independent public-input hash verification; no blanket key, path, digest pattern or scanner bypass is authorized. The actual multi-file PublicApi descriptor closure requires the ContractSet evidence clarification in wp02-04-version-identity-profile.md: preserve one namespace/major identity with every source digest, not fabricated namespaces or version changes. This explicitly authorizes the bounded metadata generator algorithm and its tests; it is not an ADP.07 input-binding exception or a change to runtime schema semantics. |

<a id="task-con-03"></a>

### CON.03 — Resource/Sync owner-body admission: closed Sync mutation allowlist + cross-owner/wrong-revision/opaque-object/forbidden-path negatives

**Outcome.** A generated/schema-derived validator enforces that Sync (registry04 §10) accepts client-origin writes only for authorized ScopeProjectMetadata and ScopeMetadata (the closed client-writable subset of AggregateBody), refusing TaskSnapshot/AutomationView/ConversationBody/AgentProfile/SkillRecord/ChatProjectRecord/MemoryRecord/PreferenceRecord as client writes (Cloud-authored-only); externalBody indirection resolves to the same allowlist before validation. Independent negative vectors cover cross-owner reference, wrong-revision precondition, opaque/unknown AggregateBody variant, and forbidden-path (non-allowlisted body kind) attempts, plus compatible unknown-response preservation. Author ScopeProjectMetadata and its additive AggregateBody variant, with cross-workspace project/session and tombstone vectors; project identity/name is never inferred from an arbitrary session. Append the ScopeProjectMetadata sample and exactly two valid positives to the generic public Foundation fixture: the standalone ScopeProjectMetadata-complete case and one AggregateBody case whose body.scopeProjectMetadata references that sample, so the existing exhaustive oneof-branch gate covers the additive variant. Add the exact C# Foundation dispatch mapping; append only the ScopeProjectMetadata seed and its exact record/field/generated-output ownership row to eng/foundation-inventory.json, and add only ScopeProjectMetadata to eng/check_foundation.py EXTRA_SEEDS so existing coverage accepts it. Do not add it to foundationTypes or alter checker logic/algorithm or generator behavior.

CON.07 repair of rows Registry04 and catalogue 01 leave without an explicit risk or step-up, fixed here so generation never infers them: the nine anonymous or refresh-token operations (identity.beginAuthentication, completeAuthentication, requestEmailCode, redeemEmailCode, refreshSession, beginRecovery, completeRecovery, completeEnrollment, listAuthProviders) use profile one-use-auth with actor kind preauth, risk R1, no step-up, approval none and egress none; identity.beginStepUp and completeStepUp are human-owner session NI R1 without step-up; identity.beginEmailChange, completeEmailChange and changePassword are session NI R3 with step-up for beginEmailChange and changePassword only; identity.requestAccountDeletion and workspace.requestDataDeletion are the only R4 rows and require step-up; device.* rows keep scope application-target and every other row scope account; every operation keeps capability null, localPresence false and egress none and only workspace.list and workspace.get are PAT eligible; Q operations carry the optional encodedBody outcome tag 4. Field profiles: product fields use the closed arcscope/companion ProductId set while platform stays a Key; WebAuthn byte fields are individually bounded (credentialId 1023, clientDataJson, attestationObject and authenticatorData 16 KiB, signature 256, userHandle 64) so the 64 KiB aggregate cannot be exceeded; password setup values accept 15 to 128 Unicode scalars; AuthChallenge requires recoveryMethod and omits method exactly for purpose recover. HTTP exceptions are two closed schema bundles: public/http/v1/browser.schema.json (strict JSON precise-value projections of the numbered Identity records for the eight /session/v1 POST routes, the bootstrap response and the OIDC provider callback query) and public/http/v1/native-auth.schema.json (PKCE authorize query, application/x-www-form-urlencoded token request bounded to 16 KiB, the redirect callback queries and the NativeSession token response); each bundle also generates a closed route table with method and path, credential, Origin/CSRF, Set-Cookie and no-store requirements, and native client/redirect pairs are exactly the contracts07 registry. Existing transfer HTTP schemas are untouched. CON.07 amendment (review of d0ebf94): Registry04 section 1 puts required scalars and enums outside a oneof under proto3 optional, so the twelve required enum fields (AuthChallenge/NativeSession/SessionView/BeginAuthentication/RequestEmailCode purpose, AuthProviderView/BeginAuthentication/BeginStepUp method, BeginRecovery method, WorkspaceView protection, DeviceView/SetTrust trust) carry explicit presence; validation is unchanged (absent and unspecified are both refused). The registry gives no bound for these fields, so the closure fixes them here and they freeze with the first release: SessionView.workspaceIds 100, DeviceView.installed 64, DeviceCapabilityView.reasons 64, scopes, capabilities and deletion/storage/job counts 200 items, WebAuthn transports 8 and allowed/excluded credentials 64, redirectUrl 4096 UTF-8 bytes, AuthProof.emailCode, recoveryCode and password and EnrollmentProof codes 128 characters versus RedeemEmailCode.code and CompleteEmailChange.code 8192 bytes (V1 has one workspace per user, so none is restrictive). The GET browser.oidcCallback route is a cross-site provider redirect that carries no Origin header: its route origin is none, and exact-configured applies only to the cookie-bearing POST routes and the bootstrap GET. Evidence class: the field oracle, boundary vectors and frozen journey expectations are offline vectors; the journeys are declarative and not executed, and the Kotlin lite runtime has no descriptors or generated validators, so the Kotlin consumer pins explicit presence on generated builders and is compiled only by the consumer harness no CI job runs. |

<a id="task-con-09"></a>

### CON.09 — Sync/resource-transfer/objects operation registry + realm-transfer.v1 + TransferTicket

**Outcome.** Add the sole canonical public Foundation arcforges.foundation.v1.TransferTicket exactly per registry04 §4: tag 1 ticketId:Id, 2 url:string, 3 expiresAt:Instant, 4 resourceId:Id, 5 range:ByteRange?, 6 maxPartBytes:uint32, reusing existing Foundation value types. It is an opaque object-facade route bound to the current session and allowed verb and never carries R2 credentials. Generate SyncService/ResourceService/TransferService with all listed operations; SyncService.pushChange/pushBatch enforce CON.03's mutation allowlist at the schema-validator boundary; realm-transfer.v1's included/excluded-roots manifest and chunked-batch (<=100 roots) semantics are schema-encoded per contracts07 §5. No other Foundation message, runtime behavior, or published compatibility baseline/version change is authorized.

<a id="task-con-10"></a>

### CON.10 — Task/approval/bridge/chat/agent/automation/search operation registry + ai-internal package

**Outcome.** All listed public services generated with exact fields and eight authorization fields. CON.10 authors ExecutionOwner (registry04), ApplicationTarget and ChatTurnProgress (annex10), ChatTurnState/ChatTurnView and the entire ToolRequest including targetApplication tag 16 in chat.proto; existing content.proto records remain canonical imports. CON.10 alone authors SessionBinding (contracts05 section 8) in internal/ai-http/v1/schema.json and exactly the 14 C# run HTTP ports authorize/claim/renew/reconcile/context/model-intent/model-outcome/settle/prepare-tools/cloud-tool/wait/finalize/stream-state/late-outcome from contracts05 section 3. OR request alternatives for those routes are represented by named Ai*Request titled oneOf roots and generated route-union codecs, never as multiple raw branch codecs left for callers to guess; branches remain exactly-one closed alternatives. These closed JSON records feed the existing ai-internal and CloudInternal packages. stream-state carries run identity, streamId, attemptId, state and nextOffset, not StreamPosition or StreamFrame. Inference ports belong solely to CON.15; public and private stream framing belongs to CON.11. The registered public SourceService policy/consent operations are authored here, importing CON.06 shared records without adding private in-process methods. Public approval.decide is the only public binding admitted by the distinct public-human-approval-decision profile: kind=proto, binding=arcforges.publicapi.v1.ApprovalService/Decide, source=public/proto/arcforges/publicapi/v1/chat.proto, scope=assistant, surface=public, sourceRule=docs/architecture/contracts/01-public-api-operations.md#rule-tk-02, idempotency=IW. Its exact eight-field authorization is capability=null, risk=verifiedApprovalProposal.effectiveRisk, approval=foregroundProposal, stepUp=verifiedApprovalProposal.stepUp, localPresence=verifiedApprovalProposal.localPresence, egress=none, patEligible=false, actorKinds=[human]. The underlying proposal is bound and validated per [TK-02](../../../architecture/contracts/01-public-api-operations.md#rule-tk-02); keep the separate local-only in-process IChatOperations.SubmitApproval human-approval-decision binding unchanged. This is static metadata admission only; no runtime authorization behavior is added.

### CON.11 — Application/history/execution/events operations (annex10's 13 additions) + EventService.Poll

**Outcome.** Generate exactly 14 public methods: ApplicationService.List/Heartbeat/Disconnect and HistoryService.BeginImport/FinalizeImport/GetImport/CancelImport in arcforges.publicapi.v1/application.proto; ExecutionService.StartTransientTurn/ReadOutput/WatchOutput/AcknowledgeOutput/PurgeTransient and EventService.Poll/Watch in arcforges.events.v1/events.proto. EventService.Poll remains the Registry04 operation with its own events.poll / resource-owner operation-scope and authorization-export rows (never alias events.watch), using the explicit Q/R1/AO pure-query profile and eight-field effective profile fixed in annex10 §8. Reuse existing ApplicationScope, RequestMeta.applicationScope tag 8, ConversationView.applicationScope tag 10 and historyMode tag 11, and HistoryMode in their current owning packages. Import CON.10 ExecutionOwner/ApplicationTarget/ChatTurnProgress and the complete ToolRequest without duplicate definitions or field appends. Generate the 17 EventService.Poll hint payloads from one event registry and the public StreamPosition/StreamFrame/OutputChunk/StreamReset/ExecutionProgress/ExecutionOutput closure. Generate the private RunStream projection in internal/proto/arcforges/cf/v1/stream.proto from annex10 section 7 as RunStreamService.Run(RunStreamRequest) returns (stream arcforges.events.v1.StreamFrame), where RunStreamRequest is execution: arcforges.publicapi.v1.ExecutionOwner = 1, attemptId: arcforges.foundation.v1.Id = 2, and generation: uint64 = 3. These fields bind an already authenticated private execution attempt; they are not a caller grant or a new public route. Reuse public StreamFrame variants; keep internal-to-public imports one-way and cap each encoded frame/output chunk at 32KiB. Exclude reserved future Hub/DeviceSso methods from active registration. TypeScript, C# and Kotlin executable consumers directly load the independently authored public fixture, consume every vector exactly once and verify generated descriptors/RPCs/fields; C# additionally directly loads the private RunStream fixture and verifies the private projection, one-way imports and frame limits. Missing, duplicate, unconsumed or unknown cases fail.

<a id="task-con-15"></a>

### CON.15 — Cloudflare-internal HTTP and D1 ExecutePlan bindings

**Outcome.** Generate the owned contracts05 object routes, Worker adapter and six inference HTTP port families as closed JSON schemas in the distinct internal/cf-http/v1/schema.json source. Include the §9 job-grant/job-authorize ports and typed bounded Worker GET/PUT object facade: stable grant/owner/fence identity, expiry <=60 seconds and no later than the owner lease, service HMAC plus matching grant, per-range/part reauthorization, and streamed parts <=8 MiB; no ambient customer bearer, arbitrary object key or raw R2 credential. CON.15 is the sole inference-family author; consume only the evidence-bound canonical CON.10 definitions without copying or changing their meaning. Register only the CF and separate D1 storage schemas with the existing generator and the two existing CloudInternal/ai-internal package identities; external-reference resolution is pinned to the exact direct CON.10 path/definition allowlist in notes and fails closed otherwise. Independent offline vectors cover duplicate dispatch, lease takeover, R2 part mismatch and stale-epoch rejection. The private D1 ExecutePlan request/reply remains a separate internal storage HTTP schema from the authoritative named-plan execution profile, with plan/parameter/result typing and fail-closed guard/error vectors; it accepts no public SQL or duplicate C# business decisions. No live Worker, provider adapter implementation, authorization handler or storage implementation is included.

Operation-scope admission is limited to the exact 17 CON.15 tuples in this route authority and the exact additive checker/test writes; source exports and oracle rows are emitted only after this Design/Plan pair is independently reviewed and merged. Preserve service actor, PAT denial, HMAC, owner/fence binding, account-scoped deletion delegation, and idempotency requirements from contracts05; this authority grants no runtime handler or general route-template expansion. Accepted-snapshot sequencing is explicit: CON.11 may consume the accepted 325-row manifest including events.poll and the 14 existing CON.10 routes; after this Design/Plan authority merges, CON.15 source rows bind to the accepted 342-row snapshot, preserving events.poll. CON.11 source completion is not a barrier to this authority pair. The registered generator emits one file set per authored bundle root, so the per-root generated-output entry and the 111 distribution rows are the exact bound; provenance, NOTICE, secret-scan and dependency-admission test updates are limited to the entries above. The CON.21/CON.22 history-test repair entries exist only because the deleted-branch commits those tests read must not be required for any Contracts pull request to pass hosted CI; they add no obligation, dependency, package identity or schema change. |

<a id="task-con-25"></a>

### CON.25 — Public ConnectorService, the local call context and the af-segment records

**Outcome.** ConnectorService is generated in the public arcforges.publicapi.v1 package (public/proto/arcforges/publicapi/v1/connector.proto) with the six connector.* operations and their public ConnectorDefinition, ConnectorConnection, ConnectorChallenge and ConnectorProof records, exact fields, authorization fields and independent positive and negative vectors in C#, TypeScript and Kotlin, so that the operation-scope gate reports the six former pending rows as registered and zero pending rows; the public records are authored in the public package because a public proto never imports internal or local schema (registry 04 section 10), and the local ConnectorBrokerService records of CON.05 stay unchanged with a shape-parity check against the public records. LocalCallContext is authored in the local platform package with its exact fields, the 4 KiB and 32-root bounds and vectors. SimulationDataSegment, SimulationSample, SimulationEvent and SimulationGap are authored as proto messages in the CON.21 simulation package with the registry field numbers and oneof presence, together with the canonical af-segment.v1 JSON profile (the segment body is canonical UTF-8 JSON, never protobuf encoder output) and golden vectors that fix the exact bytes, so that the native reader and the simulator consume one published Contracts encoding.

| Notes | Added by the planning repair of 2026-10-04 ([DLV-34](../README.md#rule-dlv-34)) after the first [WP-03.90](../../work-packages/03-contract-foundation-and-licence-split.md#rule-wp-03.90) verification (Contracts docs/wp03-90-verification.md, PR 75) found these closures ownerless: Registry04 section 5 specifies ConnectorService (ListDefinitions, ListConnections, BeginConnection, CompleteConnection, GetConnection, RevokeConnection); the six operations are exactly the six pending rows of eng/operation-scope-manifest.json (assistant scope, manifest 11), and no proto, task or Cloud runtime owned them; LocalCallContext (registry 04 section 4, annex 09) and the SimulationDataSegment/SimulationSample/SimulationEvent/SimulationGap records (registry 04 section 4) were absent with no owner. Decisions recorded here (no Design behavior change; additive Contracts closure): (1) The public ConnectorService records are authored as public records in connector.proto because the existing ConnectorDefinition, ConnectorConnection, ConnectorChallenge and ConnectorProof messages live only in the internal arcforges.local.platform.v1 package and public proto never imports internal packages (registry 04 section 10); the internal ConnectorBrokerService of CON.05 stays a private helper boundary, unchanged and already published, so the two sets are kept in shape parity by a test and unifying them is not attempted (a published local package is not rewritten). Authorization: ConnectorService management uses assistant application scope and current workspace/user connection permission (annex 10); registry 04 line 946 classifies listConnections/listDefinitions/getConnection as Q/R1/AO, beginConnection as CC/R3/FR, completeConnection as NI/R3/FR with the original one-use flow and revokeConnection as DE/R2/FR (foreground, step-up for new secret authority); the con-25.json export must record exactly these classes against the unchanged manifest rows and keep the three foreground operations out of agent, automation and extension tool generation (manifest toolAllowlist, catalogue 00); ConnectorProof carries SecretText and appears in no telemetry, fixture log or error. Open point the export author must decide and record: registry 04 line 946 says revokeConnection acts 'at expectedRev' but the section 5 request table (line 599) has only connectionId; reconcile (for example by recording that the revision precondition is the common request-envelope expectedRevision, or by an explicit planning clarification) before authoring the vectors. (2) LocalCallContext is a local proto record appended to the local platform package (annex 09 is already CON.05's authority); it imports the public ActorChain, AggregateRef and ExecutionOwner records, which a local package may do. (3) The four Simulation records ARE authored as proto messages with the registry field numbers, in the CON.21 simulation package as an additive amendment (compatible additions invalidate nothing, [DLV-36](../README.md#rule-dlv-36)), because every other registry-numbered record is a message and the generated C#, TypeScript and Kotlin shapes and the foundation inventory need the schema of record; no RPC carries them and no protobuf encoder output is ever the segment body: registry 04 section 10 fixes the af-segment.v1 body as canonical UTF-8 JSON, and this task adds the golden vectors that pin those bytes so that the native reader and the simulator ([WP-51](../../work-packages/51-arcscope-cloud-simulator.md#rule-wp-51)) share one published encoding. Not in scope and not invented: no Cloud runtime owner exists for ConnectorService and none is created here. The runtime (workspace/user connection permission, provider origin and scope allowlists, redirect and SSRF checks, and the provider callback exception /callbacks/connectors/{providerId} named in registry 04 with its HTTP schema) is a deferred follow-up for the Cloud and Extensions owners; it must be raised as a planning change when that area is scheduled, and this task makes no runtime or provider claim. The provider callback HTTP exception schema was not determined to belong to this closure and is not authored here. Use RES-contracts-dokka-profile's short allocation/generation lease after rebasing on latest Contracts main, exactly as CON.21 and CON.24 recorded: inspect the merged PROFILE/immutable successor pairs and all live durable reservations; claim exactly rN where N=active+1 and both successor paths are free, never skip, reuse or prewrite; generate only this task's Kotlin inputs and exact API page/hash sets; advance only the matching pointer; record {resource,rN,exact head/PR} in the handoff; release before review/CI. Supporting bindings (access, dependency hashes, provenance, NOTICE, Dokka, foundation inventory) follow the [ADP-07](../adoption.md#rule-adp-07) and shared-resource protocols with immutable history preserved. The .gitleaks.toml allowlist edit is authorized only as the exact three-group append named in the write scope (planning repair of 2026-10-04, after the pinned scan evidence on Contracts #83 existed); any other .gitleaks.toml or scan-allowlist change is not authorized. Not authorized: any dependency, version, closure or package-identity change, any workflow, scanner, matcher or checker-algorithm change, and any other task's rows. |
